00002         * OPTIONAL FIRST CARD AI0 (GHOST STILL) OR AI1 (RANDOM, DEFAULT).

00104          PROCEDURE DIVISION.
00105          MAIN-PARA.
00106              OPEN INPUT COMMAND-FILE.
00107              DISPLAY 'PACMAN MVS-5 LIVES AND CYCLE'.
00108              PERFORM READ-CMD.
00109              PERFORM READ-CONFIG THRU READ-CONFIG-EXIT.
00110              PERFORM INIT-GAME.
00111              MOVE 'START  ' TO WS-RESULT.   3


00112              PERFORM SHOW-STATE.
00113              PERFORM TURN-PARA
00114                  UNTIL WS-EOF = 1 OR WS-QUIT = 1 OR WS-WON = 1
00115                     OR WS-LOST = 1.
00116              IF WS-LOST = 1
00117                  DISPLAY 'LOST'
00118              ELSE IF WS-WON = 1
00119                  DISPLAY 'WIN'
00120              ELSE IF WS-QUIT = 1
00121                  DISPLAY 'QUIT'
00122              ELSE
00123                  DISPLAY 'EOF'.
00124              DISPLAY 'TURNS ' TICK-COUNT.
00125              DISPLAY 'SCORE ' SCORE.
00126              DISPLAY 'LIVES ' LIVES.
00127              CLOSE COMMAND-FILE.
00128              STOP RUN.
00129          READ-CONFIG.
00130              IF CMD-CHAR = 'A' AND WS-EOF = 0
00131                  PERFORM SET-LEVEL THRU SET-LEVEL-EXIT
00132                  PERFORM READ-CMD.
00133          READ-CONFIG-EXIT.
00134              EXIT.
00135          SET-LEVEL.
00136              IF CMD-LEVEL = '0'
00137                  MOVE 0 TO AI-LEVEL
00138              ELSE
00139                  MOVE 1 TO AI-LEVEL.
00140          SET-LEVEL-EXIT.
00141              EXIT.
00142          INIT-GAME.
00143              MOVE ' ' TO MAP-CELL (PLAYER-Y PLAYER-X).
00144              MOVE ' ' TO MAP-CELL (GHOST-Y GHOST-X).
00145              PERFORM COUNT-ROW VARYING WS-ROW FROM 1 BY 1
00146                  UNTIL WS-ROW > 10.
00147          COUNT-ROW.
00148              PERFORM COUNT-CELL THRU COUNT-CELL-EXIT
00149                  VARYING WS-COL FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-COL > 20.
00150          COUNT-CELL.
00151              IF MAP-CELL (WS-ROW WS-COL) = '.'
00152                  ADD 1 TO PELLETS-LEFT.
00153          COUNT-CELL-EXIT.
00154              EXIT.
00155          TURN-PARA.
00156              PERFORM PROCESS-CMD THRU PROCESS-CMD-EXIT.
00157              PERFORM READ-CMD.
00158          READ-CMD.
00159              READ COMMAND-FILE AT END MOVE 1 TO WS-EOF.
00160              MOVE CMD-CHAR TO WS-CMD.
00161          PROCESS-CMD.
00162              MOVE PLAYER-X TO NEW-X.
00163              MOVE PLAYER-Y TO NEW-Y.
00164              MOVE 'MOVED  ' TO WS-RESULT.
00165              IF WS-CMD = 'U' OR WS-CMD = 'u'
00166                  SUBTRACT 1 FROM NEW-Y
00167                  PERFORM TRY-MOVE
00168              ELSE IF WS-CMD = 'D' OR WS-CMD = 'd'   4


00169                  ADD 1 TO NEW-Y
00170                  PERFORM TRY-MOVE
00171              ELSE IF WS-CMD = 'L' OR WS-CMD = 'l'
00172                  SUBTRACT 1 FROM NEW-X
00173                  PERFORM TRY-MOVE
00174              ELSE IF WS-CMD = 'R' OR WS-CMD = 'r'
00175                  ADD 1 TO NEW-X
00176                  PERFORM TRY-MOVE
00177              ELSE IF WS-CMD = 'Q' OR WS-CMD = 'q'
00178                  MOVE 1 TO WS-QUIT
00179                  MOVE 'QUIT   ' TO GAME-STATE
00180              ELSE
00181                  DISPLAY 'INVALID COMMAND ' WS-CMD.
00182          PROCESS-CMD-EXIT.
00183              EXIT.
00184          TRY-MOVE.
00185              ADD 1 TO TICK-COUNT.
00186              IF NEW-X < 1 OR NEW-X > 20 OR NEW-Y < 1 OR NEW-Y > 10
00187                  MOVE 'BLOCKED' TO WS-RESULT
00188              ELSE IF MAP-CELL (NEW-Y NEW-X) = '#'
00189                  MOVE 'BLOCKED' TO WS-RESULT
00190              ELSE
00191                  MOVE NEW-X TO PLAYER-X
00192                  MOVE NEW-Y TO PLAYER-Y
00193                  PERFORM EAT-PELLET THRU EAT-PELLET-EXIT.
00194              PERFORM ROUND-END THRU ROUND-END-EXIT.
00195              PERFORM SET-STATE THRU SET-STATE-EXIT.
00196              PERFORM SHOW-STATE.
00197          ROUND-END.
00198              MOVE 0 TO WS-HIT.
00199              PERFORM CHECK-HIT THRU CHECK-HIT-EXIT.
00200              IF WS-HIT = 0 AND WS-WON = 0 AND AI-LEVEL = 1
00201                  PERFORM GHOST-MOVE THRU GHOST-MOVE-EXIT
00202                  PERFORM CHECK-HIT THRU CHECK-HIT-EXIT.
00203              IF WS-HIT = 1
00204                  PERFORM LOSE-LIFE THRU LOSE-LIFE-EXIT.
00205          ROUND-END-EXIT.
00206              EXIT.
00207          CHECK-HIT.
00208              IF GHOST-X = PLAYER-X AND GHOST-Y = PLAYER-Y
00209                  MOVE 1 TO WS-HIT.
00210          CHECK-HIT-EXIT.
00211              EXIT.
00212          LOSE-LIFE.
00213              MOVE 0 TO WS-WON.
00214              SUBTRACT 1 FROM LIVES.
00215              IF LIVES = 0
00216                  MOVE 1 TO WS-LOST
00217                  MOVE 'LOST   ' TO WS-RESULT
00218              ELSE
00219                  MOVE 'DIED   ' TO WS-RESULT
00220                  MOVE 2 TO PLAYER-X
00221                  MOVE 2 TO PLAYER-Y
00222                  MOVE 19 TO GHOST-X
00223                  MOVE 9 TO GHOST-Y
00224                  IF PELLETS-LEFT = 0
00225                      MOVE 1 TO WS-WON.   5


00226          LOSE-LIFE-EXIT.
00227              EXIT.
00228          SET-STATE.
00229              IF WS-LOST = 1
00230                  MOVE 'LOST   ' TO GAME-STATE
00231              ELSE IF WS-WON = 1
00232                  MOVE 'WON    ' TO GAME-STATE
00233              ELSE
00234                  MOVE 'RUNNING' TO GAME-STATE.
00235          SET-STATE-EXIT.
00236              EXIT.
00237          GHOST-MOVE.
00238              MOVE 0 TO CAND-N.
00239              PERFORM GHOST-PROBE THRU GHOST-PROBE-EXIT
00240                  VARYING WS-D FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-D > 4.
00241              IF CAND-N > 0
00242                  COMPUTE WS-WORK = GHOST-SEED * 75 + 74
00243                  DIVIDE WS-WORK BY 65537 GIVING WS-QUOT
00244                      REMAINDER GHOST-SEED
00245                  DIVIDE GHOST-SEED BY CAND-N GIVING WS-QUOT
00246                      REMAINDER WS-REM
00247                  ADD 1 TO WS-REM
00248                  MOVE CAND (WS-REM) TO WS-D
00249                  COMPUTE GHOST-X = GHOST-X + DIR-DX (WS-D) - 1
00250                  COMPUTE GHOST-Y = GHOST-Y + DIR-DY (WS-D) - 1.
00251          GHOST-MOVE-EXIT.
00252              EXIT.
00253          GHOST-PROBE.
00254              COMPUTE G-NX = GHOST-X + DIR-DX (WS-D) - 1.
00255              COMPUTE G-NY = GHOST-Y + DIR-DY (WS-D) - 1.
00256              IF MAP-CELL (G-NY G-NX) NOT = '#'
00257                  ADD 1 TO CAND-N
00258                  MOVE WS-D TO CAND (CAND-N).
00259          GHOST-PROBE-EXIT.
00260              EXIT.
00261          EAT-PELLET.
00262              IF MAP-CELL (PLAYER-Y PLAYER-X) = '.'
00263                  MOVE ' ' TO MAP-CELL (PLAYER-Y PLAYER-X)
00264                  ADD 10 TO SCORE
00265                  SUBTRACT 1 FROM PELLETS-LEFT.
00266              IF PELLETS-LEFT = 0
00267                  MOVE 1 TO WS-WON.
00268          EAT-PELLET-EXIT.
00269              EXIT.
00270          SHOW-STATE.
00271              MOVE TICK-COUNT TO ST-TURN.
00272              IF WS-RESULT = 'START  '
00273                  MOVE ' ' TO ST-CMD
00274              ELSE
00275                  MOVE WS-CMD TO ST-CMD.
00276              MOVE WS-RESULT TO ST-RESULT.
00277              MOVE PLAYER-X TO ST-X.
